Who are all the Presidents of
France?
 
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX onto: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX res: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX prop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
WHERE 
{
  {
    ?uri rdf:type onto:President .
    ?uri onto:country res:France .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uri rdf:type onto:Person .
    ?uri prop:title res:President_of_France .
  }
}

db26:2-en 
Which actor appeared in the most
films in a single decade?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?actor
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?actor ((FLOOR(?year/10)*10) AS ?decade) ?film
    WHERE {
      ?film a dbo:Film ;
            dbo:starring ?actor ;
            dbo:releaseDate ?date .
      BIND (xsd:integer(SUBSTR(STR(?date),1,4)) AS ?year)
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?film))
LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mithun_Chakraborty": 1
}
 
SELECT ?actor ?decade (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?filmCount) WHERE {
  ?film <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/starring> ?actor .
  ?film <http://dbpedia.org/property/year> ?year .
  FILTER(isNumeric(?year))
  BIND(FLOOR(xsd:integer(?year) / 10) AS ?decade)
}
GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
ORDER BY DESC(?filmCount)
LIMIT 1
 
{}
 
{
    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0
}

has received the highest number of
awards?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?person
WHERE {
  {
    ?person a dbo:Actor .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?person a dbo:MusicalArtist .
  }

  ?person dbo:award ?award .
}
GROUP BY ?person
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?award))
LIMIT 1

db26:5-en 
Which are the top 3 taxonomic
kingdoms that have the largest
number of endangered species?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT ?kingdom
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?species ?kingdom
    WHERE {
      ?species a dbo:Species ;
              dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
              dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
      FILTER (regex(str(?status), "Endangered|Critically endangered", "i"))
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?kingdom
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?species))
LIMIT 3
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
    "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Plantae": 1
}
 
SELECT ?kingdom (COUNT(?species) AS ?count) WHERE {
  ?species <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/conservationStatus> ?status .
  FILTER(REGEX(STR(?status), "EN", "i"))
  ?species <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/kingdom> ?kingdom .
} GROUP BY ?kingdom ORDER BY DESC(?count) LIMIT 3
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
    "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Animalia": 1,
    "2262": 1,
    "2180": 1,
    "1896": 1
}

    "set_P": 0.3333333333333333,
    "set_recall": 0.6666666666666666,
    "set_F": 0.4444444444444444

db26:20-en 
Through which cities do the rivers
originating in Yellowstone National
Park flow?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sourcePlace> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yellowstone_National_Park> .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lewiston,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tri-Cities,_Washington": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Homedale,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blackfoot,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Twin_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Burley,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/American_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Idaho_Falls,_Idaho": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?city WHERE {
  ?river <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/origin> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yellowstone_National_Park> .
  ?river <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?city .
}

db26:21-en 
Which alliance has the most member
airlines?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT ?uri
WHERE {
  ?airline a dbo:Airline .
  ?airline dbp:alliance ?uri .
}
GROUP BY ?uri
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline))
OFFSET 0 LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?alliance WHERE {
  ?airline <http://dbpedia.org/property/alliance> ?alliance .
}
GROUP BY ?alliance
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(?airline))
LIMIT 1

db26:21-es 
¿Qué alianza tiene la mayor
cantidad de aerolíneas miembros?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT ?uri
WHERE {
  ?airline a dbo:Airline .
  ?airline dbp:alliance ?uri .
}
GROUP BY ?uri
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline))
OFFSET 0 LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?alliance WHERE {
  ?airline <http://dbpedia.org/property/alliances> ?alliance .
}
GROUP BY ?alliance
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(?airline))
LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Secret_Society_of_Super_Villains": 1
}

whose tenant was the BC Lions?

  ?x a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Stadium> .
  ?uri a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Company> .
}

Name some television shows related
to the one whose opening theme was
composed by Bear McCreary.
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?x .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fear_the_Walking_Dead": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Talking_Dead": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?related WHERE {
  ?show <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/openingTheme> ?theme .
  ?theme <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/composer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?show <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?related .
}

Nombra algunos programas de
televisión relacionados con aquel
cuya música de apertura fue
compuesta por Bear McCreary.
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?x .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fear_the_Walking_Dead": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Talking_Dead": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?relatedShow WHERE {
  ?show <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?show <http://dbpedia.org/property/related> ?relatedShow .
}
 
{
    "Talking Dead": 1,
    "Fear the Walking Dead": 1
}

PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX res: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?uri rdf:type dbo:Weapon .
  ?uri dbp:designer ?x .
  res:AK-47 dbp:designer ?x .

SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
WHERE
{
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/trainer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Ring_of_Honor> .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/birthPlace> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Athlete> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Seabrook,_Maryland": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/United_States": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fontana,_California": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?athlete <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/trainer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Ring_of_Honor> .
  ?athlete <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/birthPlace> ?uri .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Seabrook,_Maryland": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/United_States": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fontana,_California": 1
}

db26:45-en 
Which saints were venerated in
Christianity?

WHERE
{

}

db26:46-en 
Which city is the radio station
located in that is a sister station
of WXME?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/RadioStation> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?city WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?station .
  ?station <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?city .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}

db26:46-es 
¿En qué ciudad está ubicada la
emisora de radio que es una
estación hermana de WXME?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/RadioStation> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?city WHERE {
  {
    <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sisterStation> ?sister .
  } UNION {
    ?sister <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sisterStation> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> .
  }
  {
    ?sister <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?city .
  } UNION {
    ?sister <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/location> ?city .
  }
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}

db26:47-en 
What is the name of the river that
has its source in Lake Itasca and
its river mouth located in Gulf of
Mexico?

WHERE {

  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/source> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lake_Itasca> .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
}

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mississippi_River": 1

  ?river <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?name .
  FILTER(LANG(?name) = "en")
}

db26:49-en 
What is the population difference
between Tokyo and Delhi?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
SELECT DISTINCT (?p2 - ?p1 AS ?result)
WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tokyo> dbo:populationTotal ?p1 .
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> dbo:populationTotal ?p2 .
}

    "3129336": 1
}

  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/populationTotal> ?delhiPop .
